    36	        self.reader.read(writer)
    37	    }
    38	
    39	    fn write(&self, reader: &mut VmReader) -> Result<usize> {
    40	        self.writer.write(reader)
    41	    }
    42

    44	        AccessMode::O_RDWR
    45	    }
    46

    48	        self.reader.metadata()
    49	    }

    39	/// A volatile file system whose data and metadata exists only in memory.
    40	pub struct RamFs {
    41	    /// The super block
    42	    sb: SuperBlock,
    43	    /// Root inode
    44	    root: Arc<RamInode>,
    45	    /// An inode allocator
    46	    inode_allocator: AtomicU64,
    47	}
    48	
    49	impl RamFs {
    50	    pub fn new() -> Arc<Self> {
    51	        Arc::new_cyclic(|weak_fs| Self {
    52	            sb: SuperBlock::new(RAMFS_MAGIC, BLOCK_SIZE, NAME_MAX),
    53	            root: Arc::new_cyclic(|weak_root| RamInode {
    54	                inner: Inner::new_dir(weak_root.clone(), weak_root.clone()),
    55	                metadata: SpinLock::new(InodeMeta::new_dir(
    56	                    mkmod!(a+rx, u+w),
    57	                    Uid::new_root(),
    58	                    Gid::new_root(),
    59	                )),
    60	                ino: ROOT_INO,
    61	                typ: InodeType::Dir,
    62	                this: weak_root.clone(),
    63	                fs: weak_fs.clone(),
    64	                extension: Extension::new(),
    65	                xattr: RamXattr::new(),
    66	            }),
    67	            inode_allocator: AtomicU64::new(ROOT_INO + 1),
    68	        })
    69	    }
    70	
    71	    fn alloc_id(&self) -> u64 {
    72	        self.inode_allocator.fetch_add(1, Ordering::SeqCst)
    73	    }
    74	}
    75	
    76	impl FileSystem for RamFs {
    77	    fn sync(&self) -> Result<()> {
    78	        // do nothing
    79	        Ok(())
    80	    }
    81	
    82	    fn root_inode(&self) -> Arc<dyn Inode> {
    83	        self.root.clone()
    84	    }
    85	
    86	    fn sb(&self) -> SuperBlock {
    87	        self.sb.clone()
    88	    }
    89	
    90	    fn flags(&self) -> FsFlags {
    91	        FsFlags::DENTRY_UNEVICTABLE
    92	    }
    93	}
    94	
    95	/// An inode of `RamFs`.
    96	struct RamInode {
    97	    /// Inode inner specifics
    98	    inner: Inner,
    99	    /// Inode metadata
   100	    metadata: SpinLock<InodeMeta>,
   101	    /// Inode number
   102	    ino: u64,
   103	    /// Type of the inode
   104	    typ: InodeType,
   105	    /// Reference to self
   106	    this: Weak<RamInode>,
   107	    /// Reference to fs
   108	    fs: Weak<RamFs>,
   109	    /// Extensions
   110	    extension: Extension,
   111	    /// Extended attributes
   112	    xattr: RamXattr,
   113	}
   114	
   115	/// Inode inner specifics.
   116	enum Inner {
   117	    Dir(RwLock<DirEntry>),
   118	    File(PageCache),
   119	    SymLink(SpinLock<String>),
   120	    Device(Arc<dyn Device>),
   121	    Socket,
   122	    NamedPipe(NamedPipe),
   123	}
   124	
   125	impl Inner {
   126	    pub fn new_dir(this: Weak<RamInode>, parent: Weak<RamInode>) -> Self {
   127	        Self::Dir(RwLock::new(DirEntry::new(this, parent)))
   128	    }
   129	
   130	    pub fn new_file(this: Weak<RamInode>) -> Self {
   131	        Self::File(PageCache::new(this).unwrap())
   132	    }
   133	
   134	    pub fn new_symlink() -> Self {
   135	        Self::SymLink(SpinLock::new(String::from("")))
   136	    }
   137	
   138	    pub fn new_device(device: Arc<dyn Device>) -> Self {
   139	        Self::Device(device)
   140	    }
   141	
   142	    pub fn new_socket() -> Self {
   143	        Self::Socket
   144	    }
   145	
   146	    pub fn new_named_pipe() -> Self {
   147	        Self::NamedPipe(NamedPipe::new().unwrap())
   148	    }
   149	
   150	    fn as_direntry(&self) -> Option<&RwLock<DirEntry>> {
   151	        match self {
   152	            Self::Dir(dir_entry) => Some(dir_entry),
   153	            _ => None,
   154	        }
   155	    }
   156	
   157	    fn as_file(&self) -> Option<&PageCache> {
   158	        match self {
   159	            Self::File(page_cache) => Some(page_cache),
   160	            _ => None,
   161	        }
   162	    }
   163	
   164	    fn as_symlink(&self) -> Option<&SpinLock<String>> {
   165	        match self {
   166	            Self::SymLink(link) => Some(link),
   167	            _ => None,
   168	        }
   169	    }
   170	
   171	    fn as_device(&self) -> Option<&Arc<dyn Device>> {
   172	        match self {
   173	            Self::Device(device) => Some(device),
   174	            _ => None,
   175	        }
   176	    }
   177	
   178	    fn as_named_pipe(&self) -> Option<&NamedPipe> {
   179	        match self {
   180	            Self::NamedPipe(pipe) => Some(pipe),
   181	            _ => None,
   182	        }
   183	    }
   184	}
   185	
   186	/// Inode metadata.
   187	#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]
   188	struct InodeMeta {
   189	    size: usize,
   190	    blocks: usize,
   191	    atime: Duration,
   192	    mtime: Duration,
   193	    ctime: Duration,
   194	    mode: InodeMode,
   195	    nlinks: usize,
   196	    uid: Uid,
   197	    gid: Gid,
   198	}
   199	
   200	impl InodeMeta {
   201	    pub fn new(mode: InodeMode, uid: Uid, gid: Gid) -> Self {
   202	        let now = now();
   203	        Self {
   204	            size: 0,
   205	            blocks: 0,
   206	            atime: now,
   207	            mtime: now,
   208	            ctime: now,
   209	            mode,
   210	            nlinks: 1,
   211	            uid,
   212	            gid,
   213	        }
   214	    }
   215	
   216	    pub fn new_dir(mode: InodeMode, uid: Uid, gid: Gid) -> Self {
   217	        let now = now();
   218	        Self {
   219	            size: NUM_SPECIAL_ENTRIES,
   220	            blocks: 1,
   221	            atime: now,
   222	            mtime: now,
   223	            ctime: now,
   224	            mode,
   225	            nlinks: NUM_SPECIAL_ENTRIES,
   226	            uid,
   227	            gid,
   228	        }
   229	    }
   230	
   231	    pub fn resize(&mut self, new_size: usize) {
   232	        self.size = new_size;
   233	        self.blocks = new_size.align_up(BLOCK_SIZE) / BLOCK_SIZE;
   234	    }
   235	
   236	    pub fn inc_size(&mut self) {
   237	        self.size += 1;
   238	        self.blocks = self.size.align_up(BLOCK_SIZE) / BLOCK_SIZE;
   239	    }
   240	
   241	    pub fn dec_size(&mut self) {
   242	        debug_assert!(self.size > 0);
   243	        self.size -= 1;
   244	        self.blocks = self.size.align_up(BLOCK_SIZE) / BLOCK_SIZE;
   245	    }
   246	
   247	    pub fn set_atime(&mut self, time: Duration) {
   248	        self.atime = time;
   249	    }
   250	
   251	    pub fn set_mtime(&mut self, time: Duration) {
   252	        self.mtime = time;
   253	    }
   254	
   255	    pub fn set_ctime(&mut self, time: Duration) {
   256	        self.ctime = time;
   257	    }
   258	
   259	    pub fn inc_nlinks(&mut self) {
   260	        self.nlinks += 1;
   261	    }
   262	
   263	    pub fn dec_nlinks(&mut self) {
   264	        debug_assert!(self.nlinks > 0);
   265	        self.nlinks -= 1;
   266	    }
   267	}
   268	
   269	/// Represents a directory entry within a `RamInode`.
   270	struct DirEntry {
   271	    children: SlotVec<(CStr256, Arc<RamInode>)>,
   272	    idx_map: HashMap<CStr256, usize>, // Used to accelerate indexing in `children`
   273	    this: Weak<RamInode>,
   274	    parent: Weak<RamInode>,
   275	}
   276	
   277	// Every directory has two special entries: "." and "..".
   278	const NUM_SPECIAL_ENTRIES: usize = 2;
   279	
   280	impl DirEntry {
   281	    fn new(this: Weak<RamInode>, parent: Weak<RamInode>) -> Self {
   282	        Self {
   283	            children: SlotVec::new(),
   284	            idx_map: HashMap::new(),
   285	            this,
   286	            parent,
   287	        }
   288	    }
   289	
   290	    fn set_parent(&mut self, parent: Weak<RamInode>) {
   291	        self.parent = parent;
   292	    }
   293	
   294	    fn contains_entry(&self, name: &str) -> bool {
   295	        if is_dot_or_dotdot(name) {
   296	            true
   297	        } else {
   298	            self.idx_map.contains_key(name.as_bytes())
   299	        }
   300	    }
   301	
   302	    fn get_entry(&self, name: &str) -> Option<(usize, Arc<RamInode>)> {
   303	        if is_dot(name) {
   304	            Some((0, self.this.upgrade().unwrap()))
   305	        } else if is_dotdot(name) {
   306	            Some((1, self.parent.upgrade().unwrap()))
   307	        } else {
   308	            let idx = *self.idx_map.get(name.as_bytes())?;
   309	            let target_inode = self
   310	                .children
   311	                .get(idx)
   312	                .map(|(name_cstr256, inode)| {
   313	                    debug_assert_eq!(name, name_cstr256.as_str().unwrap());
   314	                    inode.clone()
   315	                })
   316	                .unwrap();
   317	            Some((idx + NUM_SPECIAL_ENTRIES, target_inode))
   318	        }
   319	    }
   320	
   321	    fn append_entry(&mut self, name: &str, inode: Arc<RamInode>) -> usize {
   322	        let name = CStr256::from(name);
   323	        let idx = self.children.put((name, inode));
   324	        self.idx_map.insert(name, idx);
   325	        idx
   326	    }
   327	
   328	    fn remove_entry(&mut self, idx: usize) -> Option<(CStr256, Arc<RamInode>)> {
   329	        assert!(idx >= NUM_SPECIAL_ENTRIES);
   330	        let removed = self.children.remove(idx - NUM_SPECIAL_ENTRIES)?;
   331	        self.idx_map.remove(&removed.0);
   332	        Some(removed)
   333	    }
   334	
   335	    fn substitute_entry(
   336	        &mut self,
   337	        idx: usize,
   338	        new_entry: (CStr256, Arc<RamInode>),
   339	    ) -> Option<(CStr256, Arc<RamInode>)> {
   340	        assert!(idx >= NUM_SPECIAL_ENTRIES);
   341	        let new_name = new_entry.0;
   342	        let idx_children = idx - NUM_SPECIAL_ENTRIES;
   343	
   344	        let substitute = self.children.put_at(idx_children, new_entry)?;
   345	        let removed = self.idx_map.remove(&substitute.0);
   346	        debug_assert_eq!(removed.unwrap(), idx_children);
   347	        self.idx_map.insert(new_name, idx_children);
   348	        Some(substitute)
   349	    }
   350	
   351	    fn visit_entry(&self, idx: usize, visitor: &mut dyn DirentVisitor) -> Result<usize> {
   352	        let try_visit = |idx: &mut usize, visitor: &mut dyn DirentVisitor| -> Result<()> {
   353	            // Read the two special entries("." and "..").
   354	            if *idx == 0 {
   355	                let this_inode = self.this.upgrade().unwrap();
   356	                visitor.visit(".", this_inode.ino, this_inode.typ, *idx)?;
   357	                *idx += 1;
   358	            }
   359	            if *idx == 1 {
   360	                let parent_inode = self.parent.upgrade().unwrap();
   361	                visitor.visit("..", parent_inode.ino, parent_inode.typ, *idx)?;
   362	                *idx += 1;
   363	            }
   364	            // Read the normal child entries.
   365	            let start_idx = *idx;
   366	            for (offset_children, (name, child)) in self
   367	                .children
   368	                .idxes_and_items()
   369	                .skip_while(|(offset, _)| offset + NUM_SPECIAL_ENTRIES < start_idx)
   370	            {
   371	                let offset = offset_children + NUM_SPECIAL_ENTRIES;
   372	                visitor.visit(name.as_str().unwrap(), child.ino, child.typ, offset)?;
   373	                *idx = offset + 1;
   374	            }
   375	            Ok(())
   376	        };
   377	
   378	        let mut iterate_idx = idx;
   379	        match try_visit(&mut iterate_idx, visitor) {
   380	            Err(e) if idx == iterate_idx => Err(e),
   381	            _ => Ok(iterate_idx - idx),
   382	        }
   383	    }
   384	
   385	    fn is_empty_children(&self) -> bool {
   386	        self.children.is_empty()
   387	    }
   388	}
   389	
   390	impl RamInode {
   391	    fn new_dir(
   392	        fs: &Arc<RamFs>,
   393	        mode: InodeMode,
   394	        uid: Uid,
   395	        gid: Gid,
   396	        parent: &Weak<RamInode>,
   397	    ) -> Arc<Self> {
   398	        Arc::new_cyclic(|weak_self| RamInode {
   399	            inner: Inner::new_dir(weak_self.clone(), parent.clone()),
   400	            metadata: SpinLock::new(InodeMeta::new_dir(mode, uid, gid)),
   401	            ino: fs.alloc_id(),
   402	            typ: InodeType::Dir,
   403	            this: weak_self.clone(),
   404	            fs: Arc::downgrade(fs),
   405	            extension: Extension::new(),
   406	            xattr: RamXattr::new(),
   407	        })
   408	    }
   409	
   410	    fn new_file(fs: &Arc<RamFs>, mode: InodeMode, uid: Uid, gid: Gid) -> Arc<Self> {
   411	        Arc::new_cyclic(|weak_self| RamInode {
   412	            inner: Inner::new_file(weak_self.clone()),
   413	            metadata: SpinLock::new(InodeMeta::new(mode, uid, gid)),
   414	            ino: fs.alloc_id(),
   415	            typ: InodeType::File,
   416	            this: weak_self.clone(),
   417	            fs: Arc::downgrade(fs),
   418	            extension: Extension::new(),
   419	            xattr: RamXattr::new(),
   420	        })
   421	    }
   422	
   423	    fn new_file_detached(mode: InodeMode, uid: Uid, gid: Gid) -> Arc<Self> {
   424	        Arc::new_cyclic(|weak_self| RamInode {
   425	            inner: Inner::new_file(weak_self.clone()),
   426	            metadata: SpinLock::new(InodeMeta::new(mode, uid, gid)),
   427	            ino: weak_self.as_ptr() as u64,
   428	            typ: InodeType::File,
   429	            this: weak_self.clone(),
   430	            fs: Weak::new(),
   431	            extension: Extension::new(),
   432	            xattr: RamXattr::new(),
   433	        })
   434	    }
   435	
   436	    fn new_symlink(fs: &Arc<RamFs>, mode: InodeMode, uid: Uid, gid: Gid) -> Arc<Self> {
   437	        Arc::new_cyclic(|weak_self| RamInode {
   438	            inner: Inner::new_symlink(),
   439	            metadata: SpinLock::new(InodeMeta::new(mode, uid, gid)),
   440	            ino: fs.alloc_id(),
   441	            typ: InodeType::SymLink,
   442	            this: weak_self.clone(),
   443	            fs: Arc::downgrade(fs),
   444	            extension: Extension::new(),
   445	            xattr: RamXattr::new(),
   446	        })
   447	    }
   448	
   449	    fn new_device(
   450	        fs: &Arc<RamFs>,
   451	        mode: InodeMode,
   452	        uid: Uid,
   453	        gid: Gid,
   454	        device: Arc<dyn Device>,
   455	    ) -> Arc<Self> {
   456	        Arc::new_cyclic(|weak_self| RamInode {
   457	            inner: Inner::new_device(device.clone()),
   458	            metadata: SpinLock::new(InodeMeta::new(mode, uid, gid)),
   459	            ino: fs.alloc_id(),
   460	            typ: InodeType::from(device.type_()),
   461	            this: weak_self.clone(),
   462	            fs: Arc::downgrade(fs),
   463	            extension: Extension::new(),
   464	            xattr: RamXattr::new(),
   465	        })
   466	    }
   467	
   468	    fn new_socket(fs: &Arc<RamFs>, mode: InodeMode, uid: Uid, gid: Gid) -> Arc<Self> {
   469	        Arc::new_cyclic(|weak_self| RamInode {
   470	            inner: Inner::new_socket(),
   471	            metadata: SpinLock::new(InodeMeta::new(mode, uid, gid)),
   472	            ino: fs.alloc_id(),
   473	            typ: InodeType::Socket,
   474	            this: weak_self.clone(),
   475	            fs: Arc::downgrade(fs),
   476	            extension: Extension::new(),
   477	            xattr: RamXattr::new(),
   478	        })
   479	    }
   480	
   481	    fn new_named_pipe(fs: &Arc<RamFs>, mode: InodeMode, uid: Uid, gid: Gid) -> Arc<Self> {
   482	        Arc::new_cyclic(|weak_self| RamInode {
   483	            inner: Inner::new_named_pipe(),
   484	            metadata: SpinLock::new(InodeMeta::new(mode, uid, gid)),
   485	            ino: fs.alloc_id(),
   486	            typ: InodeType::NamedPipe,
   487	            this: weak_self.clone(),
   488	            fs: Arc::downgrade(fs),
   489	            extension: Extension::new(),
   490	            xattr: RamXattr::new(),
   491	        })
   492	    }
   493	
   494	    fn find(&self, name: &str) -> Result<Arc<Self>> {
   495	        if self.typ != InodeType::Dir {
   496	            return_errno_with_message!(Errno::ENOTDIR, "self is not dir");
   497	        }
   498	
   499	        let (_, inode) = self
   500	            .inner
   501	            .as_direntry()
   502	            .unwrap()
   503	            .read()
   504	            .get_entry(name)
   505	            .ok_or(Error::new(Errno::ENOENT))?;
   506	        Ok(inode)
   507	    }
   508	}
   509	
   510	impl PageCacheBackend for RamInode {
   511	    fn read_page_async(&self, _idx: usize, frame: &CachePage) -> Result<BioWaiter> {
   512	        // Initially, any block/page in a RamFs inode contains all zeros
   513	        frame.writer().fill_zeros(frame.size());
   514	        Ok(BioWaiter::new())
   515	    }
   516	
   517	    fn write_page_async(&self, _idx: usize, _frame: &CachePage) -> Result<BioWaiter> {
   518	        // do nothing
   519	        Ok(BioWaiter::new())
   520	    }
   521	
   522	    fn npages(&self) -> usize {
   523	        self.metadata.lock().blocks
   524	    }
   525	}
   526	
   527	impl Inode for RamInode {
   528	    fn page_cache(&self) -> Option<Vmo<Full>> {
   529	        self.inner
   530	            .as_file()
   531	            .map(|page_cache| page_cache.pages().dup())
   532	    }
   533	
   534	    fn read_at(&self, offset: usize, writer: &mut VmWriter) -> Result<usize> {
   535	        let read_len = {
   536	            match &self.inner {
   537	                Inner::File(page_cache) => {
   538	                    let (offset, read_len) = {
   539	                        let file_size = self.size();
   540	                        let start = file_size.min(offset);
   541	                        let end = file_size.min(offset + writer.avail());
   542	                        (start, end - start)
   543	                    };
   544	                    page_cache.pages().read(offset, writer)?;
   545	                    read_len
   546	                }
   547	                Inner::Device(device) => {
   548	                    device.read(writer)?
   549	                    // Typically, devices like "/dev/zero" or "/dev/null" do not require modifying
   550	                    // timestamps here. Please adjust this behavior accordingly if there are special devices.
   551	                }
   552	                Inner::NamedPipe(named_pipe) => named_pipe.read(writer)?,
   553	                _ => return_errno_with_message!(Errno::EISDIR, "read is not supported"),
   554	            }
   555	        };
   556	
   557	        if self.typ == InodeType::File {
   558	            self.set_atime(now());
   559	        }
   560	        Ok(read_len)
   561	    }
   562	
   563	    fn read_direct_at(&self, offset: usize, writer: &mut VmWriter) -> Result<usize> {
   564	        self.read_at(offset, writer)
   565	    }
   566	
   567	    fn write_at(&self, offset: usize, reader: &mut VmReader) -> Result<usize> {
   568	        let written_len = match self.typ {
   569	            InodeType::File => {
   570	                let page_cache = self.inner.as_file().unwrap();
   571	
   572	                let file_size = self.size();
   573	                let write_len = reader.remain();
   574	                let new_size = offset + write_len;
   575	                let should_expand_size = new_size > file_size;
   576	                let new_size_aligned = new_size.align_up(BLOCK_SIZE);
   577	                if should_expand_size {
   578	                    page_cache.resize(new_size_aligned)?;
   579	                }
   580	                page_cache.pages().write(offset, reader)?;
   581	
   582	                let now = now();
   583	                let mut inode_meta = self.metadata.lock();
   584	                inode_meta.set_mtime(now);
   585	                inode_meta.set_ctime(now);
   586	                if should_expand_size {
   587	                    inode_meta.size = new_size;
   588	                    inode_meta.blocks = new_size_aligned / BLOCK_SIZE;
   589	                }
   590	                write_len
   591	            }
   592	            InodeType::CharDevice | InodeType::BlockDevice => {
   593	                let device = self.inner.as_device().unwrap();
   594	                device.write(reader)?
   595	                // Typically, devices like "/dev/zero" or "/dev/null" do not require modifying
   596	                // timestamps here. Please adjust this behavior accordingly if there are special devices.
   597	            }
   598	            InodeType::NamedPipe => {
   599	                let named_pipe = self.inner.as_named_pipe().unwrap();
   600	                named_pipe.write(reader)?
   601	            }
   602	            _ => return_errno_with_message!(Errno::EISDIR, "write is not supported"),
   603	        };
   604	        Ok(written_len)
   605	    }
   606	
   607	    fn write_direct_at(&self, offset: usize, reader: &mut VmReader) -> Result<usize> {
   608	        self.write_at(offset, reader)
   609	    }
   610	
   611	    fn size(&self) -> usize {
   612	        self.metadata.lock().size
   613	    }
   614	
   615	    fn resize(&self, new_size: usize) -> Result<()> {
   616	        if self.typ != InodeType::File {
   617	            return_errno_with_message!(Errno::EISDIR, "not regular file");
   618	        }
   619	
   620	        let file_size = self.size();
   621	        if file_size == new_size {
   622	            return Ok(());
   623	        }
   624	
   625	        let page_cache = self.inner.as_file().unwrap();
   626	        page_cache.resize(new_size)?;
   627	
   628	        let now = now();
   629	        let mut inode_meta = self.metadata.lock();
   630	        inode_meta.set_mtime(now);
   631	        inode_meta.set_ctime(now);
   632	        inode_meta.resize(new_size);
   633	        Ok(())
   634	    }
   635	
   636	    fn atime(&self) -> Duration {
   637	        self.metadata.lock().atime
   638	    }
   639	
   640	    fn set_atime(&self, time: Duration) {
   641	        self.metadata.lock().set_atime(time);
   642	    }
   643	
   644	    fn mtime(&self) -> Duration {
   645	        self.metadata.lock().mtime
   646	    }
   647	
   648	    fn set_mtime(&self, time: Duration) {
   649	        self.metadata.lock().set_mtime(time);
   650	    }
   651	
   652	    fn ctime(&self) -> Duration {
   653	        self.metadata.lock().ctime
   654	    }
   655	
   656	    fn set_ctime(&self, time: Duration) {
   657	        self.metadata.lock().set_ctime(time);
   658	    }
   659	
   660	    fn ino(&self) -> u64 {
   661	        self.ino
   662	    }
   663	
   664	    fn type_(&self) -> InodeType {
   665	        self.typ
   666	    }
   667	
   668	    fn mode(&self) -> Result<InodeMode> {
   669	        Ok(self.metadata.lock().mode)
   670	    }
   671	
   672	    fn set_mode(&self, mode: InodeMode) -> Result<()> {
   673	        let mut inode_meta = self.metadata.lock();
   674	        inode_meta.mode = mode;
   675	        inode_meta.set_ctime(now());
   676	        Ok(())
   677	    }
   678	
   679	    fn owner(&self) -> Result<Uid> {
   680	        Ok(self.metadata.lock().uid)
   681	    }
   682	
   683	    fn set_owner(&self, uid: Uid) -> Result<()> {
   684	        let mut inode_meta = self.metadata.lock();
   685	        inode_meta.uid = uid;
   686	        inode_meta.set_ctime(now());
   687	        Ok(())
   688	    }
   689	
   690	    fn group(&self) -> Result<Gid> {
   691	        Ok(self.metadata.lock().gid)
   692	    }
   693	
   694	    fn set_group(&self, gid: Gid) -> Result<()> {
   695	        let mut inode_meta = self.metadata.lock();
   696	        inode_meta.gid = gid;
   697	        inode_meta.set_ctime(now());
   698	        Ok(())
   699	    }
   700	
   701	    fn mknod(&self, name: &str, mode: InodeMode, type_: MknodType) -> Result<Arc<dyn Inode>> {
   702	        if name.len() > NAME_MAX {
   703	            return_errno!(Errno::ENAMETOOLONG);
   704	        }
   705	        if self.typ != InodeType::Dir {
   706	            return_errno_with_message!(Errno::ENOTDIR, "self is not dir");
   707	        }
   708	
   709	        let self_dir = self.inner.as_direntry().unwrap().upread();
   710	        if self_dir.contains_entry(name) {
   711	            return_errno_with_message!(Errno::EEXIST, "entry exists");
   712	        }
   713	
   714	        let new_inode = match type_ {
   715	            MknodType::CharDevice(device) | MknodType::BlockDevice(device) => RamInode::new_device(
   716	                &self.fs.upgrade().unwrap(),
   717	                mode,
   718	                Uid::new_root(),
   719	                Gid::new_root(),
   720	                device,
   721	            ),
   722	            MknodType::NamedPipe => RamInode::new_named_pipe(
   723	                &self.fs.upgrade().unwrap(),
   724	                mode,
   725	                Uid::new_root(),
   726	                Gid::new_root(),
   727	            ),
   728	        };
   729	
   730	        let mut self_dir = self_dir.upgrade();
   731	        self_dir.append_entry(name, new_inode.clone());
   732	        drop(self_dir);
   733	
   734	        self.metadata.lock().inc_size();
   735	        Ok(new_inode)
   736	    }
   737	
   738	    fn as_device(&self) -> Option<Arc<dyn Device>> {
   739	        if !self.typ.is_device() {
   740	            return None;
   741	        }
   742	        self.inner.as_device().cloned()
   743	    }
   744	
   745	    fn create(&self, name: &str, type_: InodeType, mode: InodeMode) -> Result<Arc<dyn Inode>> {
   746	        if name.len() > NAME_MAX {
   747	            return_errno!(Errno::ENAMETOOLONG);
   748	        }
   749	        if self.typ != InodeType::Dir {
   750	            return_errno_with_message!(Errno::ENOTDIR, "self is not dir");
   751	        }
   752	
   753	        let self_dir = self.inner.as_direntry().unwrap().upread();
   754	        if self_dir.contains_entry(name) {
   755	            return_errno_with_message!(Errno::EEXIST, "entry exists");
   756	        }
   757	
   758	        let fs = self.fs.upgrade().unwrap();
   759	        let new_inode = match type_ {
   760	            InodeType::File => RamInode::new_file(&fs, mode, Uid::new_root(), Gid::new_root()),
   761	            InodeType::SymLink => {
   762	                RamInode::new_symlink(&fs, mode, Uid::new_root(), Gid::new_root())
   763	            }
   764	            InodeType::Socket => RamInode::new_socket(&fs, mode, Uid::new_root(), Gid::new_root()),
   765	            InodeType::Dir => {
   766	                RamInode::new_dir(&fs, mode, Uid::new_root(), Gid::new_root(), &self.this)
   767	            }
   768	            _ => {
   769	                panic!("unsupported inode type");
   770	            }
   771	        };
   772	
   773	        let mut self_dir = self_dir.upgrade();
   774	        self_dir.append_entry(name, new_inode.clone());
   775	        drop(self_dir);
   776	
   777	        let now = now();
   778	        let mut inode_meta = self.metadata.lock();
   779	        inode_meta.set_mtime(now);
   780	        inode_meta.set_ctime(now);
   781	        inode_meta.inc_size();
   782	        if type_ == InodeType::Dir {
   783	            inode_meta.inc_nlinks();
   784	        }
   785	
   786	        Ok(new_inode)
   787	    }
   788	
   789	    fn readdir_at(&self, offset: usize, visitor: &mut dyn DirentVisitor) -> Result<usize> {
   790	        if self.typ != InodeType::Dir {
   791	            return_errno_with_message!(Errno::ENOTDIR, "self is not dir");
   792	        }
   793	
   794	        let cnt = self
   795	            .inner
   796	            .as_direntry()
   797	            .unwrap()
   798	            .read()
   799	            .visit_entry(offset, visitor)?;
   800	
   801	        self.set_atime(now());
   802	
   803	        Ok(cnt)
   804	    }
   805	
   806	    fn link(&self, old: &Arc<dyn Inode>, name: &str) -> Result<()> {
   807	        if !Arc::ptr_eq(&self.fs(), &old.fs()) {
   808	            return_errno_with_message!(Errno::EXDEV, "not same fs");
   809	        }
   810	        if self.typ != InodeType::Dir {
   811	            return_errno_with_message!(Errno::ENOTDIR, "self is not dir");
   812	        }
   813	
   814	        let old = old
   815	            .downcast_ref::<RamInode>()
   816	            .ok_or(Error::new(Errno::EXDEV))?;
   817	        if old.typ == InodeType::Dir {
   818	            return_errno_with_message!(Errno::EPERM, "old is a dir");
   819	        }
   820	
   821	        let mut self_dir = self.inner.as_direntry().unwrap().write();
   822	        if self_dir.contains_entry(name) {
   823	            return_errno_with_message!(Errno::EEXIST, "entry exist");
   824	        }
   825	        self_dir.append_entry(name, old.this.upgrade().unwrap());
   826	        drop(self_dir);
   827	
   828	        let now = now();
   829	        let mut self_meta = self.metadata.lock();
   830	        self_meta.set_mtime(now);
   831	        self_meta.set_ctime(now);
   832	        self_meta.inc_size();
   833	        drop(self_meta);
   834	
   835	        let mut old_meta = old.metadata.lock();
   836	        old_meta.inc_nlinks();
   837	        old_meta.set_ctime(now);
   838	
   839	        Ok(())
   840	    }
   841	
   842	    fn unlink(&self, name: &str) -> Result<()> {
   843	        if is_dot_or_dotdot(name) {
   844	            return_errno_with_message!(Errno::EISDIR, "unlink . or ..");
   845	        }
   846	
   847	        let target = self.find(name)?;
   848	        if target.typ == InodeType::Dir {
   849	            return_errno_with_message!(Errno::EISDIR, "unlink on dir");
   850	        }
   851	
   852	        // When we got the lock, the dir may have been modified by another thread
   853	        let mut self_dir = self.inner.as_direntry().unwrap().write();
   854	        let (idx, new_target) = self_dir.get_entry(name).ok_or(Error::new(Errno::ENOENT))?;
   855	        if !Arc::ptr_eq(&new_target, &target) {
   856	            return_errno!(Errno::ENOENT);
   857	        }
   858	        self_dir.remove_entry(idx);
   859	        drop(self_dir);
   860	
   861	        let now = now();
   862	        let mut self_meta = self.metadata.lock();
   863	        self_meta.dec_size();
   864	        self_meta.set_mtime(now);
   865	        self_meta.set_ctime(now);
   866	        drop(self_meta);
   867	        let mut target_meta = target.metadata.lock();
   868	        target_meta.dec_nlinks();
   869	        target_meta.set_ctime(now);
   870	
   871	        Ok(())
   872	    }
   873	
   874	    fn rmdir(&self, name: &str) -> Result<()> {
   875	        if is_dot(name) {
   876	            return_errno_with_message!(Errno::EINVAL, "rmdir on .");
   877	        }
   878	        if is_dotdot(name) {
   879	            return_errno_with_message!(Errno::ENOTEMPTY, "rmdir on ..");
   880	        }
   881	
   882	        let target = self.find(name)?;
   883	        if target.typ != InodeType::Dir {
   884	            return_errno_with_message!(Errno::ENOTDIR, "rmdir on not dir");
   885	        }
   886	
   887	        // When we got the lock, the dir may have been modified by another thread
   888	        let (mut self_dir, target_dir) = write_lock_two_direntries_by_ino(
   889	            (self.ino, self.inner.as_direntry().unwrap()),
   890	            (target.ino, target.inner.as_direntry().unwrap()),
   891	        );
   892	        if !target_dir.is_empty_children() {
   893	            return_errno_with_message!(Errno::ENOTEMPTY, "dir not empty");
   894	        }
   895	        let (idx, new_target) = self_dir.get_entry(name).ok_or(Error::new(Errno::ENOENT))?;
   896	        if !Arc::ptr_eq(&new_target, &target) {
   897	            return_errno!(Errno::ENOENT);
   898	        }
   899	        self_dir.remove_entry(idx);
   900	        drop(self_dir);
   901	        drop(target_dir);
   902	
   903	        let now = now();
   904	        let mut self_meta = self.metadata.lock();
   905	        self_meta.dec_size();
   906	        self_meta.dec_nlinks();
   907	        self_meta.set_mtime(now);
   908	        self_meta.set_ctime(now);
   909	        drop(self_meta);
   910	        let mut target_meta = target.metadata.lock();
   911	        target_meta.dec_nlinks();
   912	        target_meta.dec_nlinks();
   913	
   914	        Ok(())
   915	    }
   916	
   917	    fn lookup(&self, name: &str) -> Result<Arc<dyn Inode>> {
   918	        let inode = self.find(name)?;
   919	        Ok(inode as _)
   920	    }
   921	
   922	    fn rename(&self, old_name: &str, target: &Arc<dyn Inode>, new_name: &str) -> Result<()> {
   923	        if is_dot_or_dotdot(old_name) {
   924	            return_errno_with_message!(Errno::EISDIR, "old_name is . or ..");
   925	        }
   926	        if is_dot_or_dotdot(new_name) {
   927	            return_errno_with_message!(Errno::EISDIR, "new_name is . or ..");
   928	        }
   929	
   930	        let target = target
   931	            .downcast_ref::<RamInode>()
   932	            .ok_or(Error::new(Errno::EXDEV))?;
   933	
   934	        if !Arc::ptr_eq(&self.fs(), &target.fs()) {
   935	            return_errno_with_message!(Errno::EXDEV, "not same fs");
   936	        }
   937	        if self.typ != InodeType::Dir {
   938	            return_errno_with_message!(Errno::ENOTDIR, "self is not dir");
   939	        }
   940	        if target.typ != InodeType::Dir {
   941	            return_errno_with_message!(Errno::ENOTDIR, "target is not dir");
   942	        }
   943	
   944	        // Perform necessary checks to ensure that `dst_inode` can be replaced by `src_inode`.
   945	        let check_replace_inode =
   946	            |src_inode: &Arc<RamInode>, dst_inode: &Arc<RamInode>| -> Result<()> {
   947	                if src_inode.ino == dst_inode.ino {
   948	                    return Ok(());
   949	                }
   950	
   951	                match (src_inode.typ, dst_inode.typ) {
   952	                    (InodeType::Dir, InodeType::Dir) => {
   953	                        if !dst_inode
   954	                            .inner
   955	                            .as_direntry()
   956	                            .unwrap()
   957	                            .read()
   958	                            .is_empty_children()
   959	                        {
   960	                            return_errno_with_message!(Errno::ENOTEMPTY, "dir not empty");
   961	                        }
   962	                    }
   963	                    (InodeType::Dir, _) => {
   964	                        return_errno_with_message!(Errno::ENOTDIR, "old is not dir");
   965	                    }
   966	                    (_, InodeType::Dir) => {
   967	                        return_errno_with_message!(Errno::EISDIR, "new is dir");
   968	                    }
   969	                    _ => {}
   970	                }
   971	                Ok(())
   972	            };
   973	
   974	        // Rename in the same directory
   975	        if self.ino == target.ino {
   976	            let mut self_dir = self.inner.as_direntry().unwrap().write();
   977	            let (src_idx, src_inode) = self_dir
   978	                .get_entry(old_name)
   979	                .ok_or(Error::new(Errno::ENOENT))?;
   980	            let is_dir = src_inode.typ == InodeType::Dir;
   981	
   982	            if let Some((dst_idx, dst_inode)) = self_dir.get_entry(new_name) {
   983	                check_replace_inode(&src_inode, &dst_inode)?;
   984	                self_dir.remove_entry(dst_idx);
   985	                self_dir.substitute_entry(src_idx, (CStr256::from(new_name), src_inode.clone()));
   986	                drop(self_dir);
   987	
   988	                let now = now();
   989	                let mut self_meta = self.metadata.lock();
   990	                self_meta.dec_size();
   991	                if is_dir {
   992	                    self_meta.dec_nlinks();
   993	                }
   994	                self_meta.set_mtime(now);
   995	                self_meta.set_ctime(now);
   996	                drop(self_meta);
   997	                src_inode.set_ctime(now);
   998	                dst_inode.set_ctime(now);
   999	            } else {
  1000	                self_dir.substitute_entry(src_idx, (CStr256::from(new_name), src_inode.clone()));
  1001	                drop(self_dir);
  1002	                let now = now();
  1003	                let mut self_meta = self.metadata.lock();
  1004	                self_meta.set_mtime(now);
  1005	                self_meta.set_ctime(now);
  1006	                drop(self_meta);
  1007	                src_inode.set_ctime(now);
  1008	            }
  1009	        }
  1010	        // Or rename across different directories
  1011	        else {
  1012	            let (mut self_dir, mut target_dir) = write_lock_two_direntries_by_ino(
  1013	                (self.ino, self.inner.as_direntry().unwrap()),
  1014	                (target.ino, target.inner.as_direntry().unwrap()),
  1015	            );
  1016	            let self_inode_arc = self.this.upgrade().unwrap();
  1017	            let target_inode_arc = target.this.upgrade().unwrap();
  1018	            let (src_idx, src_inode) = self_dir
  1019	                .get_entry(old_name)
  1020	                .ok_or(Error::new(Errno::ENOENT))?;
  1021	            // Avoid renaming a directory to a subdirectory of itself
  1022	            if Arc::ptr_eq(&src_inode, &target_inode_arc) {
  1023	                return_errno!(Errno::EINVAL);
  1024	            }
  1025	            let is_dir = src_inode.typ == InodeType::Dir;
  1026	
  1027	            if let Some((dst_idx, dst_inode)) = target_dir.get_entry(new_name) {
  1028	                // Avoid renaming a subdirectory to a directory.
  1029	                if Arc::ptr_eq(&self_inode_arc, &dst_inode) {
  1030	                    return_errno!(Errno::ENOTEMPTY);
  1031	                }
  1032	                check_replace_inode(&src_inode, &dst_inode)?;
  1033	                self_dir.remove_entry(src_idx);
  1034	                target_dir.remove_entry(dst_idx);
  1035	                target_dir.append_entry(new_name, src_inode.clone());
  1036	                drop(self_dir);
  1037	                drop(target_dir);
  1038	
  1039	                let now = now();
  1040	                let mut self_meta = self.metadata.lock();
  1041	                self_meta.dec_size();
  1042	                if is_dir {
  1043	                    self_meta.dec_nlinks();
  1044	                }
  1045	                self_meta.set_mtime(now);
  1046	                self_meta.set_ctime(now);
  1047	                drop(self_meta);
  1048	                let mut target_meta = target.metadata.lock();
  1049	                target_meta.set_mtime(now);
  1050	                target_meta.set_ctime(now);
  1051	                drop(target_meta);
  1052	                dst_inode.set_ctime(now);
  1053	                src_inode.set_ctime(now);
  1054	            } else {
  1055	                self_dir.remove_entry(src_idx);
  1056	                target_dir.append_entry(new_name, src_inode.clone());
  1057	                drop(self_dir);
  1058	                drop(target_dir);
  1059	
  1060	                let now = now();
  1061	                let mut self_meta = self.metadata.lock();
  1062	                self_meta.dec_size();
  1063	                if is_dir {
  1064	                    self_meta.dec_nlinks();
  1065	                }
  1066	                self_meta.set_mtime(now);
  1067	                self_meta.set_ctime(now);
  1068	                drop(self_meta);
  1069	
  1070	                let mut target_meta = target.metadata.lock();
  1071	                target_meta.inc_size();
  1072	                if is_dir {
  1073	                    target_meta.inc_nlinks();
  1074	                }
  1075	                target_meta.set_mtime(now);
  1076	                target_meta.set_ctime(now);
  1077	                drop(target_meta);
  1078	                src_inode.set_ctime(now);
  1079	            }
  1080	
  1081	            if is_dir {
  1082	                src_inode
  1083	                    .inner
  1084	                    .as_direntry()
  1085	                    .unwrap()
  1086	                    .write()
  1087	                    .set_parent(target.this.clone());
  1088	            }
  1089	        }
  1090	        Ok(())
  1091	    }
  1092	
  1093	    fn read_link(&self) -> Result<String> {
  1094	        if self.typ != InodeType::SymLink {
  1095	            return_errno_with_message!(Errno::EINVAL, "self is not symlink");
  1096	        }
  1097	
  1098	        let link = self.inner.as_symlink().unwrap().lock();
  1099	        Ok(link.clone())
  1100	    }
  1101	
  1102	    fn write_link(&self, target: &str) -> Result<()> {
  1103	        if self.typ != InodeType::SymLink {
  1104	            return_errno_with_message!(Errno::EINVAL, "self is not symlink");
  1105	        }
  1106	
  1107	        let mut link = self.inner.as_symlink().unwrap().lock();
  1108	        *link = String::from(target);
  1109	        drop(link);
  1110	
  1111	        // Symlink's metadata.blocks should be 0, so just set the size.
  1112	        self.metadata.lock().size = target.len();
  1113	        Ok(())
  1114	    }
  1115	
  1116	    fn metadata(&self) -> Metadata {
  1117	        let rdev = self
  1118	            .inner
  1119	            .as_device()
  1120	            .map(|device| device.id().as_encoded_u64())
  1121	            .unwrap_or(0);
  1122	        let inode_metadata = self.metadata.lock();
  1123	        Metadata {
  1124	            dev: 0,
  1125	            ino: self.ino as _,
  1126	            size: inode_metadata.size,
  1127	            blk_size: BLOCK_SIZE,
  1128	            blocks: inode_metadata.blocks,
  1129	            atime: inode_metadata.atime,
  1130	            mtime: inode_metadata.mtime,
  1131	            ctime: inode_metadata.ctime,
  1132	            type_: self.typ,
  1133	            mode: inode_metadata.mode,
  1134	            nlinks: inode_metadata.nlinks,
  1135	            uid: inode_metadata.uid,
  1136	            gid: inode_metadata.gid,
  1137	            rdev,
  1138	        }
  1139	    }
  1140	
  1141	    fn poll(&self, mask: IoEvents, poller: Option<&mut PollHandle>) -> IoEvents {
  1142	        if !self.typ.is_device() {
  1143	            return (IoEvents::IN | IoEvents::OUT) & mask;
  1144	        }
  1145	
  1146	        let device = self
  1147	            .inner
  1148	            .as_device()
  1149	            .expect("[Internal error] self.typ is device, while self.inner is not");
  1150	        device.poll(mask, poller)
  1151	    }
  1152	
  1153	    fn fs(&self) -> Arc<dyn FileSystem> {
  1154	        Weak::upgrade(&self.fs).unwrap()
  1155	    }
  1156	
  1157	    fn fallocate(&self, mode: FallocMode, offset: usize, len: usize) -> Result<()> {
  1158	        if self.typ != InodeType::File {
  1159	            return_errno_with_message!(Errno::EISDIR, "not regular file");
  1160	        }
  1161	
  1162	        // The support for flags is consistent with Linux
  1163	        match mode {
  1164	            FallocMode::Allocate => {
  1165	                let new_size = offset + len;
  1166	                if new_size > self.size() {
  1167	                    self.resize(new_size)?;
  1168	                }
  1169	                Ok(())
  1170	            }
  1171	            FallocMode::AllocateKeepSize => {
  1172	                // Do nothing
  1173	                Ok(())
  1174	            }
  1175	            FallocMode::PunchHoleKeepSize => {
  1176	                let file_size = self.size();
  1177	                if offset >= file_size {
  1178	                    return Ok(());
  1179	                }
  1180	                let range = offset..file_size.min(offset + len);
  1181	                // TODO: Think of a more light-weight approach
  1182	                self.inner.as_file().unwrap().fill_zeros(range)
  1183	            }
  1184	            _ => {
  1185	                return_errno_with_message!(
  1186	                    Errno::EOPNOTSUPP,
  1187	                    "fallocate with the specified flags is not supported"
  1188	                );
  1189	            }
  1190	        }
  1191	    }
  1192	
  1193	    fn ioctl(&self, cmd: IoctlCmd, arg: usize) -> Result<i32> {
  1194	        if let Some(device) = self.inner.as_device() {
  1195	            return device.ioctl(cmd, arg);
  1196	        }
  1197	        return_errno_with_message!(Errno::EINVAL, "ioctl is not supported");
  1198	    }
  1199	
  1200	    fn is_seekable(&self) -> bool {
  1201	        !matches!(
  1202	            self.typ,
  1203	            InodeType::NamedPipe | InodeType::CharDevice | InodeType::Dir | InodeType::Socket
  1204	        )
  1205	    }
  1206	
  1207	    fn extension(&self) -> Option<&Extension> {
  1208	        Some(&self.extension)
  1209	    }
  1210	
  1211	    fn set_xattr(
  1212	        &self,
  1213	        name: XattrName,
  1214	        value_reader: &mut VmReader,
  1215	        flags: XattrSetFlags,
  1216	    ) -> Result<()> {
  1217	        RamXattr::check_file_type_for_xattr(self.typ)?;
  1218	        self.check_permission(Permission::MAY_WRITE)?;
  1219	        self.xattr.set(name, value_reader, flags)
  1220	    }
  1221	
  1222	    fn get_xattr(&self, name: XattrName, value_writer: &mut VmWriter) -> Result<usize> {
  1223	        RamXattr::check_file_type_for_xattr(self.typ)
  1224	            .map_err(|_| Error::with_message(Errno::ENODATA, "no available xattrs"))?;
  1225	        self.check_permission(Permission::MAY_READ)?;
  1226	        self.xattr.get(name, value_writer)
  1227	    }
  1228	
  1229	    fn list_xattr(&self, namespace: XattrNamespace, list_writer: &mut VmWriter) -> Result<usize> {
  1230	        if RamXattr::check_file_type_for_xattr(self.typ).is_err() {
  1231	            return Ok(0);
  1232	        }
  1233	        if self.check_permission(Permission::MAY_ACCESS).is_err() {
  1234	            return Ok(0);
  1235	        }
  1236	        self.xattr.list(namespace, list_writer)
  1237	    }
  1238	
  1239	    fn remove_xattr(&self, name: XattrName) -> Result<()> {
  1240	        RamXattr::check_file_type_for_xattr(self.typ)?;
  1241	        self.check_permission(Permission::MAY_WRITE)?;
  1242	        self.xattr.remove(name)
  1243	    }
  1244	}
  1245	
  1246	/// Creates a RAM inode that is detached from any `RamFs`.
  1247	///
  1248	// TODO: Add "anonymous inode fs" and link the inode to it.
  1249	pub fn new_detached_inode(mode: InodeMode, uid: Uid, gid: Gid) -> Arc<dyn Inode> {
  1250	    RamInode::new_file_detached(mode, uid, gid)
  1251	}
  1252	
  1253	fn write_lock_two_direntries_by_ino<'a>(
  1254	    this: (u64, &'a RwLock<DirEntry>),
  1255	    other: (u64, &'a RwLock<DirEntry>),
  1256	) -> (
  1257	    RwLockWriteGuard<'a, DirEntry, PreemptDisabled>,
  1258	    RwLockWriteGuard<'a, DirEntry, PreemptDisabled>,
  1259	) {
  1260	    if this.0 < other.0 {
  1261	        let this = this.1.write();
  1262	        let other = other.1.write();
  1263	        (this, other)
  1264	    } else {
  1265	        let other = other.1.write();
  1266	        let this = this.1.write();
  1267	        (this, other)
  1268	    }
  1269	}
  1270	
  1271	fn now() -> Duration {
  1272	    RealTimeCoarseClock::get().read_time()
  1273	}
  1274	
  1275	pub(super) struct RamFsType;
  1276	
  1277	impl FsType for RamFsType {
  1278	    fn name(&self) -> &'static str {
  1279	        "ramfs"
  1280	    }
  1281	
  1282	    fn properties(&self) -> FsProperties {
  1283	        FsProperties::empty()
  1284	    }
  1285	
  1286	    fn create(
  1287	        &self,
  1288	        _args: Option<CString>,
  1289	        _disk: Option<Arc<dyn aster_block::BlockDevice>>,
  1290	    ) -> Result<Arc<dyn FileSystem>> {
  1291	        Ok(RamFs::new())
  1292	    }
  1293	
  1294	    fn sysnode(&self) -> Option<Arc<dyn aster_systree::SysNode>> {
  1295	        None
  1296	    }
  1297	}

===== kernel/src/fs/path/mod.rs =====
     1	// SPDX-License-Identifier: MPL-2.0
     2	
     3	//! Form file paths within and across FSes with dentries and mount points.
     4	
     5	use core::time::Duration;
     6	
     7	use inherit_methods_macro::inherit_methods;
     8	pub use mount::{Mount, MountPropType};
     9	pub use mount_namespace::MountNamespace;
    10	
    11	use crate::{
    12	    fs::{
    13	        inode_handle::InodeHandle,
    14	        path::dentry::{Dentry, DentryKey},
    15	        utils::{
    16	            CreationFlags, FileSystem, Inode, InodeMode, InodeType, Metadata, MknodType, OpenArgs,
    17	            Permission, StatusFlags, XattrName, XattrNamespace, XattrSetFlags, NAME_MAX,
    18	        },
    19	    },
    20	    prelude::*,
    21	    process::{Gid, Uid},
    22	};
    23	
    24	mod dentry;
    25	mod mount;
    26	mod mount_namespace;
    27	
    28	/// A `Path` is used to represent an exact location in the VFS tree.
    29	///
    30	/// Each `Path` corresponds to a node in the VFS tree, and a single node
    31	/// may have multiple `Path` instances referencing it due to mount operations.
    32	#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
    33	pub struct Path {
    34	    mount: Arc<Mount>,
    35	    dentry: Arc<Dentry>,
    36	}
    37	
    38	impl Path {
    39	    /// Creates a new `Path` to represent the root directory of a file system.
    40	    pub fn new_fs_root(mount: Arc<Mount>) -> Self {
    41	        let inner = mount.root_dentry().clone();
    42	        Self::new(mount, inner)
    43	    }
    44	
    45	    /// Creates a new `Path` to represent the child directory of a file system.
    46	    pub fn new_fs_child(&self, name: &str, type_: InodeType, mode: InodeMode) -> Result<Self> {
    47	        if self
    48	            .inode()
    49	            .check_permission(Permission::MAY_WRITE)
    50	            .is_err()
    51	        {
    52	            return_errno!(Errno::EACCES);
    53	        }
    54	        let new_child_dentry = self.dentry.create(name, type_, mode)?;
    55	        Ok(Self::new(self.mount.clone(), new_child_dentry))
    56	    }
    57	
    58	    fn new(mount: Arc<Mount>, dentry: Arc<Dentry>) -> Self {
    59	        Self { mount, dentry }
    60	    }
    61	
    62	    /// Gets the mount node of current `Path`.
    63	    pub fn mount_node(&self) -> &Arc<Mount> {
    64	        &self.mount
    65	    }
    66	
    67	    /// Returns true if the current `Path` is the root of its mount.
    68	    pub(super) fn is_mount_root(&self) -> bool {
    69	        Arc::ptr_eq(&self.dentry, self.mount.root_dentry())
    70	    }
    71	
    72	    /// Lookups the target `Path` given the `name`.
    73	    pub fn lookup(&self, name: &str) -> Result<Self> {
    74	        if self.type_() != InodeType::Dir {
    75	            return_errno_with_message!(Errno::ENOTDIR, "the path is not a directory");
    76	        }
    77	        if self.inode().check_permission(Permission::MAY_EXEC).is_err() {
    78	            return_errno_with_message!(Errno::EACCES, "the path cannot be looked up");
    79	        }
    80	        if name.len() > NAME_MAX {
    81	            return_errno_with_message!(Errno::ENAMETOOLONG, "the path name is too long");
    82	        }
    83	
    84	        let target_path = if is_dot(name) {
    85	            self.this()
    86	        } else if is_dotdot(name) {
    87	            self.effective_parent().unwrap_or_else(|| self.this())
    88	        } else {
    89	            let target_inner_opt = self.dentry.lookup_via_cache(name)?;
    90	            match target_inner_opt {
    91	                Some(target_inner) => Self::new(self.mount.clone(), target_inner),
    92	                None => {
    93	                    let target_inner = self.dentry.lookup_via_fs(name)?;
    94	                    Self::new(self.mount.clone(), target_inner)
    95	                }
    96	            }
    97	        };
    98	
    99	        Ok(target_path.get_top_path())
   100	    }
   101	
   102	    /// Gets the absolute path.
   103	    ///
   104	    /// It will resolve the mountpoint automatically.
   105	    pub fn abs_path(&self) -> String {
   106	        let mut path_name = self.effective_name();
   107	        let mut current_dir = self.this();
   108	
   109	        while let Some(parent_dir) = current_dir.effective_parent() {
   110	            path_name = {
   111	                let parent_name = parent_dir.effective_name();
   112	                if parent_name != "/" {
   113	                    parent_name + "/" + &path_name
   114	                } else {
   115	                    parent_name + &path_name
   116	                }
   117	            };
   118	            current_dir = parent_dir;
   119	        }
   120	
   121	        debug_assert!(path_name.starts_with('/'));
   122	        path_name
   123	    }
   124	
   125	    /// Gets the effective name of the `Path`.
   126	    ///
   127	    /// If it is the root of a mount, it will go up to the mountpoint
   128	    /// to get the name of the mountpoint recursively.
   129	    fn effective_name(&self) -> String {
   130	        if !self.is_mount_root() {
   131	            return self.dentry.name();
   132	        }
   133	
   134	        let Some(parent) = self.mount.parent() else {
   135	            return self.dentry.name();
   136	        };
   137	        let Some(mountpoint) = self.mount.mountpoint() else {
   138	            return self.dentry.name();
   139	        };
   140	
   141	        let mount_parent = Self::new(parent.upgrade().unwrap(), mountpoint);
   142	        mount_parent.effective_name()
   143	    }
   144	
   145	    /// Gets the effective parent of the `Path`.
   146	    ///
   147	    /// If it is the root of a mount, it will go up to the mountpoint
   148	    /// to get the parent of the mountpoint recursively.
   149	    fn effective_parent(&self) -> Option<Self> {
   150	        if !self.is_mount_root() {
   151	            return Some(Self::new(self.mount.clone(), self.dentry.parent().unwrap()));
   152	        }
   153	
   154	        let parent = self.mount.parent()?;
   155	        let mountpoint = self.mount.mountpoint()?;
   156	
   157	        let mount_parent = Self::new(parent.upgrade().unwrap(), mountpoint);
   158	        mount_parent.effective_parent()
   159	    }
   160	
   161	    /// Gets the top `Path` of the current.
   162	    ///
   163	    /// Used when different file systems are mounted on the same mount point.
   164	    ///
   165	    /// For example, first `mount /dev/sda1 /mnt` and then `mount /dev/sda2 /mnt`.
   166	    /// After the second mount is completed, the content of the first mount will be overridden.
   167	    /// We need to recursively obtain the top `Path`.
   168	    pub(super) fn get_top_path(mut self) -> Self {
   169	        while self.dentry.is_mountpoint() {
   170	            if let Some(child_mount) = self.mount.get(&self.dentry) {
   171	                let inner = child_mount.root_dentry().clone();
   172	                self = Self::new(child_mount, inner);
   173	            } else {
   174	                break;
   175	            }
   176	        }
   177	
   178	        self
   179	    }
   180	
   181	    /// Finds the corresponding `Path` in the given mount namespace.
   182	    pub(super) fn find_corresponding_mount(&self, mnt_ns: &Arc<MountNamespace>) -> Option<Self> {
   183	        let corresponding_mount = self.mount.find_corresponding_mount(mnt_ns)?;
   184	        let corresponding_path = Self::new(corresponding_mount, self.dentry.clone());
   185	
   186	        Some(corresponding_path)
   187	    }
   188	
   189	    fn this(&self) -> Self {
   190	        self.clone()
   191	    }
   192	}
   193	
   194	impl Path {
   195	    /// Mounts a filesystem at the current path.
   196	    ///
   197	    /// This method attaches a given filesystem to the VFS tree at the location
   198	    /// represented by `self`. The current path becomes the mountpoint for the new
   199	    /// filesystem.
   200	    ///
   201	    /// Returns the newly created child mount on success.
   202	    ///
   203	    /// # Errors
   204	    ///
   205	    /// Returns `ENOTDIR` if the path is not a directory.
   206	    /// Returns `EINVAL` if attempting to mount on root or if the path is not
   207	    /// in the current mount namespace.
   208	    pub fn mount(&self, fs: Arc<dyn FileSystem>, ctx: &Context) -> Result<Arc<Mount>> {
   209	        if self.type_() != InodeType::Dir {
   210	            return_errno_with_message!(Errno::ENOTDIR, "the path is not a directory");
   211	        }
   212	
   213	        if self.effective_parent().is_none() {
   214	            return_errno_with_message!(Errno::EINVAL, "the root cannot be mounted on");
   215	        }
   216	
   217	        let current_ns_proxy = ctx.thread_local.borrow_ns_proxy();
   218	        let current_mnt_ns = current_ns_proxy.unwrap().mnt_ns();
   219	        if !current_mnt_ns.owns(&self.mount) {
   220	            return_errno_with_message!(Errno::EINVAL, "the path is not in this mount namespace");
   221	        }
   222	
   223	        let child_mount = self.mount.do_mount(fs, &self.dentry)?;
   224	
   225	        Ok(child_mount)
   226	    }
   227	
   228	    /// Unmounts the filesystem mounted at the current path.
   229	    ///
   230	    /// Returns the unmounted child mount on success.
   231	    ///
   232	    /// # Errors
   233	    ///
   234	    /// Returns `EINVAL` in the following cases:
   235	    /// - The current path is not a mount root.
   236	    /// - The mount of the current path is the root mount.
   237	    /// - The current path is not in the current mount namespace.
   238	    pub fn unmount(&self, ctx: &Context) -> Result<Arc<Mount>> {
   239	        if !self.is_mount_root() {
   240	            return_errno_with_message!(Errno::EINVAL, "the path is not a mount root");
   241	        }
   242	
   243	        let Some(mountpoint) = self.mount.mountpoint() else {
   244	            return_errno_with_message!(Errno::EINVAL, "the root mount cannot be unmounted");
   245	        };
   246	
   247	        let current_ns_proxy = ctx.thread_local.borrow_ns_proxy();
   248	        let current_mnt_ns = current_ns_proxy.unwrap().mnt_ns();
   249	        if !current_mnt_ns.owns(&self.mount) {
   250	            return_errno_with_message!(Errno::EINVAL, "the path is not in this mount namespace");
   251	        }
   252	
   253	        let parent_mount = self.mount.parent().unwrap().upgrade().unwrap();
   254	        let child_mount = parent_mount.do_unmount(&mountpoint)?;
   255	
   256	        Ok(child_mount)
   257	    }
   258	
   259	    /// Creates a bind mount from the current path to the destination path.
   260	    ///
   261	    /// Creates a new mount tree that mirrors either the root mount (non-recursive)
   262	    /// or the entire mount subtree (recursive), and attaches it to the destination path.
   263	    ///
   264	    /// # Errors
   265	    ///
   266	    /// Returns `ENOTDIR` if the `dst_path` is not a directory.
   267	    /// Returns `EINVAL` if either source or destination path is not in the
   268	    /// current mount namespace.
   269	    pub fn bind_mount_to(&self, dst_path: &Self, recursive: bool, ctx: &Context) -> Result<()> {
   270	        let current_ns_proxy = ctx.thread_local.borrow_ns_proxy();
   271	        let current_mnt_ns = current_ns_proxy.unwrap().mnt_ns();
   272	        if !current_mnt_ns.owns(&self.mount) {
   273	            return_errno_with_message!(
   274	                Errno::EINVAL,
   275	                "the source path is not in this mount namespace"
   276	            );
   277	        }
   278	        if !current_mnt_ns.owns(&dst_path.mount) {
   279	            return_errno_with_message!(
   280	                Errno::EINVAL,
   281	                "the destination path is not in this mount namespace"
   282	            );
   283	        }
   284	
   285	        let new_mount = self.mount.clone_mount_tree(&self.dentry, None, recursive);
   286	        new_mount.graft_mount_tree(dst_path)?;
   287	        Ok(())
   288	    }
   289	
   290	    /// Moves a mount tree from the current path to the destination path.
   291	    ///
   292	    /// # Errors
   293	    ///
   294	    /// Returns `ENOTDIR` if the `dst_path` is not a directory.
   295	    /// Returns `EINVAL` in the following cases:
   296	    /// - The current path is not a mount root.
   297	    /// - The mount of the current path is the root mount.
   298	    /// - Either source or destination path is not in the current mount namespace
   299	    pub fn move_mount_to(&self, dst_path: &Self, ctx: &Context) -> Result<()> {
   300	        if !self.is_mount_root() {
   301	            return_errno_with_message!(Errno::EINVAL, "the path is not a mount root");
   302	        };
   303	        if self.mount_node().parent().is_none() {
   304	            return_errno_with_message!(Errno::EINVAL, "the root mount can not be moved");
   305	        }
   306	
   307	        let current_ns_proxy = ctx.thread_local.borrow_ns_proxy();
   308	        let current_mnt_ns = current_ns_proxy.unwrap().mnt_ns();
   309	        if !current_mnt_ns.owns(&self.mount) {
   310	            return_errno_with_message!(
   311	                Errno::EINVAL,
   312	                "the source path is not in this mount namespace"
   313	            );
   314	        }
   315	        if !current_mnt_ns.owns(&dst_path.mount) {
   316	            return_errno_with_message!(
   317	                Errno::EINVAL,
   318	                "the destination path is not in this mount namespace"
   319	            );
   320	        }
   321	
   322	        self.mount.graft_mount_tree(dst_path)
   323	    }
   324	
   325	    /// Sets the propagation type of the mount of this `Path`.
   326	    pub fn set_mount_propagation(
   327	        &self,
   328	        prop: MountPropType,
   329	        recursive: bool,
   330	        ctx: &Context,
   331	    ) -> Result<()> {
   332	        if !self.is_mount_root() {
   333	            return_errno_with_message!(Errno::EINVAL, "the path is not a mount root");
   334	        };
   335	
   336	        let current_ns_proxy = ctx.thread_local.borrow_ns_proxy();
   337	        let current_mnt_ns = current_ns_proxy.unwrap().mnt_ns();
   338	        if !current_mnt_ns.owns(&self.mount) {
   339	            return_errno_with_message!(Errno::EINVAL, "the path is not in this mount namespace");
   340	        }
   341	
   342	        self.mount.set_propagation(prop, recursive);
   343	
   344	        Ok(())
   345	    }
   346	
   347	    /// Creates a `Path` by making an inode of the `type_` with the `mode`.
   348	    pub fn mknod(&self, name: &str, mode: InodeMode, type_: MknodType) -> Result<Self> {
   349	        let inner = self.dentry.mknod(name, mode, type_)?;
   350	        Ok(Self::new(self.mount.clone(), inner))
   351	    }
   352	
   353	    /// Links a new name for the `Path`.
   354	    pub fn link(&self, old: &Self, name: &str) -> Result<()> {
   355	        if !Arc::ptr_eq(&old.mount, &self.mount) {
   356	            return_errno_with_message!(Errno::EXDEV, "the operation cannot cross mounts");
   357	        }
   358	
   359	        self.dentry.link(&old.dentry, name)
   360	    }
   361	
   362	    /// Renames a `Path` to the new `Path` by `rename()` the inner inode.
   363	    pub fn rename(&self, old_name: &str, new_dir: &Self, new_name: &str) -> Result<()> {
   364	        if !Arc::ptr_eq(&self.mount, &new_dir.mount) {
   365	            return_errno_with_message!(Errno::EXDEV, "the operation cannot cross mounts");
   366	        }
   367	
   368	        self.dentry.rename(old_name, &new_dir.dentry, new_name)
   369	    }
   370	
   371	    /// Opens the `Path` with the given `OpenArgs`.
   372	    ///
   373	    /// Returns an `InodeHandle` on success.
   374	    pub fn open(&self, open_args: OpenArgs) -> Result<InodeHandle> {
   375	        let inode = self.inode();
   376	        let inode_type = inode.type_();
   377	        let creation_flags = &open_args.creation_flags;
   378	
   379	        match inode_type {
   380	            InodeType::NamedPipe => {
   381	                warn!("named pipes don't support additional operation when opening");
   382	                debug!("the named pipe is opened with {:?}", open_args);
   383	            }
   384	            InodeType::SymLink => {
   385	                if creation_flags.contains(CreationFlags::O_NOFOLLOW)
   386	                    && !open_args.status_flags.contains(StatusFlags::O_PATH)
   387	                {
   388	                    return_errno_with_message!(Errno::ELOOP, "the file is a symlink");
   389	                }
   390	            }
   391	            _ => {}
   392	        }
   393	
   394	        if creation_flags.contains(CreationFlags::O_CREAT)
   395	            && creation_flags.contains(CreationFlags::O_EXCL)
   396	        {
   397	            return_errno_with_message!(Errno::EEXIST, "the file already exists");
   398	        }
   399	        if creation_flags.contains(CreationFlags::O_DIRECTORY) && inode_type != InodeType::Dir {
   400	            return_errno_with_message!(
   401	                Errno::ENOTDIR,
   402	                "O_DIRECTORY is specified but the file is not a directory"
   403	            );
   404	        }
   405	
   406	        if inode_type.is_regular_file() && creation_flags.contains(CreationFlags::O_TRUNC) {
   407	            self.resize(0)?;
   408	        }
   409	        InodeHandle::new(self.clone(), open_args.access_mode, open_args.status_flags)
   410	    }
   411	}
   412	
   413	#[inherit_methods(from = "self.dentry")]
   414	impl Path {
   415	    pub fn unlink(&self, name: &str) -> Result<()>;
   416	    pub fn rmdir(&self, name: &str) -> Result<()>;
   417	    pub fn fs(&self) -> Arc<dyn FileSystem>;
   418	    pub fn sync_all(&self) -> Result<()>;
   419	    pub fn sync_data(&self) -> Result<()>;
   420	    pub fn metadata(&self) -> Metadata;
   421	    pub fn type_(&self) -> InodeType;
   422	    pub fn mode(&self) -> Result<InodeMode>;
   423	    pub fn set_mode(&self, mode: InodeMode) -> Result<()>;
   424	    pub fn size(&self) -> usize;
   425	    pub fn resize(&self, size: usize) -> Result<()>;
   426	    pub fn owner(&self) -> Result<Uid>;
   427	    pub fn set_owner(&self, uid: Uid) -> Result<()>;
   428	    pub fn group(&self) -> Result<Gid>;
   429	    pub fn set_group(&self, gid: Gid) -> Result<()>;
   430	    pub fn atime(&self) -> Duration;
   431	    pub fn set_atime(&self, time: Duration);
   432	    pub fn mtime(&self) -> Duration;
   433	    pub fn set_mtime(&self, time: Duration);
   434	    pub fn ctime(&self) -> Duration;
   435	    pub fn set_ctime(&self, time: Duration);
   436	    pub fn key(&self) -> DentryKey;
   437	    pub fn inode(&self) -> &Arc<dyn Inode>;
   438	    pub fn is_mountpoint(&self) -> bool;
   439	    pub fn set_xattr(
   440	        &self,
   441	        name: XattrName,
   442	        value_reader: &mut VmReader,
   443	        flags: XattrSetFlags,
   444	    ) -> Result<()>;
   445	    pub fn get_xattr(&self, name: XattrName, value_writer: &mut VmWriter) -> Result<usize>;
   446	    pub fn list_xattr(
   447	        &self,
   448	        namespace: XattrNamespace,
   449	        list_writer: &mut VmWriter,
   450	    ) -> Result<usize>;
   451	    pub fn remove_xattr(&self, name: XattrName) -> Result<()>;
   452	}
   453	
   454	/// Checks if the file name is ".", indicating it's the current directory.
   455	pub const fn is_dot(filename: &str) -> bool {
   456	    let name_bytes = filename.as_bytes();
   457	    name_bytes.len() == 1 && name_bytes[0] == DOT_BYTE
   458	}
   459	
   460	/// Checks if the file name is "..", indicating it's the parent directory.
   461	pub const fn is_dotdot(filename: &str) -> bool {
   462	    let name_bytes = filename.as_bytes();
   463	    name_bytes.len() == 2 && name_bytes[0] == DOT_BYTE && name_bytes[1] == DOT_BYTE
   464	}
   465	
   466	/// Checks if the file name is "." or "..", indicating it's the current or parent directory.
   467	pub const fn is_dot_or_dotdot(filename: &str) -> bool {
   468	    let name_bytes = filename.as_bytes();
   469	    let name_len = name_bytes.len();
   470	    if name_len == 1 {
   471	        name_bytes[0] == DOT_BYTE
   472	    } else if name_len == 2 {
   473	        name_bytes[0] == DOT_BYTE && name_bytes[1] == DOT_BYTE
   474	    } else {
   475	        false
   476	    }
   477	}
   478	
   479	const DOT_BYTE: u8 = b'.';

===== kernel/src/fs/pipe.rs =====
     1	// SPDX-License-Identifier: MPL-2.0
     2	
     3	use core::sync::atomic::{AtomicU32, Ordering};
     4	
     5	use super::{
     6	    file_handle::FileLike,
     7	    utils::{mkmod, AccessMode, Endpoint, EndpointState, InodeType, Metadata, StatusFlags},
     8	};
     9	use crate::{
    10	    events::IoEvents,
    11	    prelude::*,
    12	    process::{
    13	        signal::{PollHandle, Pollable},
    14	        Gid, Uid,
    15	    },
    16	    time::clocks::RealTimeCoarseClock,
    17	    util::ring_buffer::{RbConsumer, RbProducer, RingBuffer},
    18	};
    19	
    20	const DEFAULT_PIPE_BUF_SIZE: usize = 65536;
    21	
    22	/// Maximum number of bytes guaranteed to be written to a pipe atomically.
    23	///
    24	/// If the number of bytes to be written is less than the threshold, the write must be atomic.
    25	/// A non-blocking atomic write may fail with `EAGAIN`, even if there is room for a partial write.
    26	/// In other words, a partial write is not allowed for an atomic write.
    27	///
    28	/// For more details, see the description of `PIPE_BUF` in
    29	/// <https://man7.org/linux/man-pages/man7/pipe.7.html>.
    30	#[cfg(not(ktest))]
    31	const PIPE_BUF: usize = 4096;
    32	#[cfg(ktest)]
    33	const PIPE_BUF: usize = 2;
    34	
    35	pub fn new_pair() -> Result<(Arc<PipeReader>, Arc<PipeWriter>)> {
    36	    new_pair_with_capacity(DEFAULT_PIPE_BUF_SIZE)
    37	}
    38	
    39	fn new_pair_with_capacity(capacity: usize) -> Result<(Arc<PipeReader>, Arc<PipeWriter>)> {
    40	    let (producer, consumer) = RingBuffer::new(capacity).split();
    41	    let (producer_state, consumer_state) =
    42	        Endpoint::new_pair(EndpointState::default(), EndpointState::default());
    43	
    44	    Ok((
    45	        PipeReader::new(consumer, consumer_state, StatusFlags::empty())?,
    46	        PipeWriter::new(producer, producer_state, StatusFlags::empty())?,
    47	    ))
    48	}
    49	
    50	pub struct PipeReader {
    51	    consumer: Mutex<RbConsumer<u8>>,
    52	    state: Endpoint<EndpointState>,
    53	    status_flags: AtomicU32,
    54	}
    55	
    56	impl PipeReader {
    57	    fn new(
    58	        consumer: RbConsumer<u8>,
    59	        state: Endpoint<EndpointState>,
    60	        status_flags: StatusFlags,
    61	    ) -> Result<Arc<Self>> {
    62	        check_status_flags(status_flags)?;
    63	
    64	        Ok(Arc::new(Self {
    65	            consumer: Mutex::new(consumer),
    66	            state,
    67	            status_flags: AtomicU32::new(status_flags.bits()),
    68	        }))
    69	    }
    70	
    71	    fn try_read(&self, writer: &mut VmWriter) -> Result<usize> {
    72	        let read = || {
    73	            let mut consumer = self.consumer.lock();
    74	            consumer.read_fallible(writer)
    75	        };
    76	
    77	        self.state.read_with(read)
    78	    }
    79	
    80	    fn check_io_events(&self) -> IoEvents {
    81	        let mut events = IoEvents::empty();
    82	        if self.state.is_peer_shutdown() {
    83	            events |= IoEvents::HUP;
    84	        }
    85	        if !self.consumer.lock().is_empty() {
    86	            events |= IoEvents::IN;
    87	        }
    88	        events
    89	    }
    90	}
    91	
    92	impl Pollable for PipeReader {
    93	    fn poll(&self, mask: IoEvents, poller: Option<&mut PollHandle>) -> IoEvents {
    94	        self.state
    95	            .poll_with(mask, poller, || self.check_io_events())
    96	    }
    97	}
    98	
    99	impl FileLike for PipeReader {
   100	    fn read(&self, writer: &mut VmWriter) -> Result<usize> {
   101	        if !writer.has_avail() {
   102	            // Even the peer endpoint (`PipeWriter`) has been closed, reading an empty buffer is
   103	            // still fine.
   104	            return Ok(0);
   105	        }
   106	
   107	        if self.status_flags().contains(StatusFlags::O_NONBLOCK) {
   108	            self.try_read(writer)
   109	        } else {
   110	            self.wait_events(IoEvents::IN, None, || self.try_read(writer))
   111	        }
   112	    }
   113	
   114	    fn status_flags(&self) -> StatusFlags {
   115	        StatusFlags::from_bits_truncate(self.status_flags.load(Ordering::Relaxed))
   116	    }
   117	
   118	    fn set_status_flags(&self, new_flags: StatusFlags) -> Result<()> {
   119	        check_status_flags(new_flags)?;
   120	
   121	        self.status_flags.store(new_flags.bits(), Ordering::Relaxed);
   122	        Ok(())
   123	    }
   124	
   125	    fn access_mode(&self) -> AccessMode {
   126	        AccessMode::O_RDONLY
   127	    }
   128	
   129	    fn metadata(&self) -> Metadata {
   130	        // This is a dummy implementation.
   131	        // TODO: Add "PipeFS" and link `PipeReader` to it.
   132	        let now = RealTimeCoarseClock::get().read_time();
   133	        Metadata {
   134	            dev: 0,
   135	            ino: 0,
   136	            size: 0,
   137	            blk_size: 0,
   138	            blocks: 0,
   139	            atime: now,
   140	            mtime: now,
   141	            ctime: now,
   142	            type_: InodeType::NamedPipe,
   143	            mode: mkmod!(u+r),
   144	            nlinks: 1,
   145	            uid: Uid::new_root(),
   146	            gid: Gid::new_root(),
   147	            rdev: 0,
   148	        }
   149	    }
   150	}
   151	
   152	impl Drop for PipeReader {
   153	    fn drop(&mut self) {
   154	        self.state.peer_shutdown();
   155	    }
   156	}
   157	
   158	pub struct PipeWriter {
   159	    producer: Mutex<RbProducer<u8>>,
   160	    state: Endpoint<EndpointState>,
   161	    status_flags: AtomicU32,
   162	}
   163	
   164	impl PipeWriter {
   165	    fn new(
   166	        producer: RbProducer<u8>,
   167	        state: Endpoint<EndpointState>,
   168	        status_flags: StatusFlags,
   169	    ) -> Result<Arc<Self>> {
   170	        check_status_flags(status_flags)?;
   171	
   172	        Ok(Arc::new(Self {
   173	            producer: Mutex::new(producer),
   174	            state,
   175	            status_flags: AtomicU32::new(status_flags.bits()),
   176	        }))
   177	    }
   178	
   179	    fn try_write(&self, reader: &mut VmReader) -> Result<usize> {
   180	        let write = || {
   181	            let mut producer = self.producer.lock();
   182	            if reader.remain() <= PIPE_BUF && producer.free_len() < reader.remain() {
   183	                // No sufficient space for an atomic write
   184	                return Ok(0);
   185	            }
   186	            producer.write_fallible(reader)
   187	        };
   188	
   189	        self.state.write_with(write)
   190	    }
   191	
   192	    fn check_io_events(&self) -> IoEvents {
   193	        if self.state.is_shutdown() {
   194	            IoEvents::ERR | IoEvents::OUT
   195	        } else if self.producer.lock().free_len() >= PIPE_BUF {
   196	            IoEvents::OUT
   197	        } else {
   198	            IoEvents::empty()
   199	        }
   200	    }
   201	}
   202	
   203	impl Pollable for PipeWriter {
   204	    fn poll(&self, mask: IoEvents, poller: Option<&mut PollHandle>) -> IoEvents {
   205	        self.state
   206	            .poll_with(mask, poller, || self.check_io_events())
   207	    }
   208	}
   209	
   210	impl FileLike for PipeWriter {
   211	    fn write(&self, reader: &mut VmReader) -> Result<usize> {
   212	        if !reader.has_remain() {
   213	            // Even the peer endpoint (`PipeReader`) has been closed, writing an empty buffer is
   214	            // still fine.
   215	            return Ok(0);
   216	        }
   217	
   218	        if self.status_flags().contains(StatusFlags::O_NONBLOCK) {
   219	            self.try_write(reader)
   220	        } else {
   221	            self.wait_events(IoEvents::OUT, None, || self.try_write(reader))
   222	        }
   223	    }
   224	
   225	    fn status_flags(&self) -> StatusFlags {
   226	        StatusFlags::from_bits_truncate(self.status_flags.load(Ordering::Relaxed))
   227	    }
   228	
   229	    fn set_status_flags(&self, new_flags: StatusFlags) -> Result<()> {
   230	        check_status_flags(new_flags)?;
   231	
   232	        self.status_flags.store(new_flags.bits(), Ordering::Relaxed);
   233	        Ok(())
   234	    }
   235	
   236	    fn access_mode(&self) -> AccessMode {
   237	        AccessMode::O_WRONLY
   238	    }
   239	
   240	    fn metadata(&self) -> Metadata {
   241	        // This is a dummy implementation.
   242	        // TODO: Add "PipeFS" and link `PipeWriter` to it.
   243	        let now = RealTimeCoarseClock::get().read_time();
   244	        Metadata {
   245	            dev: 0,
   246	            ino: 0,
   247	            size: 0,
   248	            blk_size: 0,
   249	            blocks: 0,
   250	            atime: now,
   251	            mtime: now,
   252	            ctime: now,
   253	            type_: InodeType::NamedPipe,
   254	            mode: mkmod!(u+w),
   255	            nlinks: 1,
   256	            uid: Uid::new_root(),
   257	            gid: Gid::new_root(),
   258	            rdev: 0,
   259	        }
   260	    }
   261	}
   262	
   263	fn check_status_flags(status_flags: StatusFlags) -> Result<()> {
   264	    if status_flags.contains(StatusFlags::O_DIRECT) {
   265	        // "O_DIRECT .. Older kernels that do not support this flag will indicate this via an
   266	        // EINVAL error."
   267	        //
   268	        // See <https://man7.org/linux/man-pages/man2/pipe.2.html>.
   269	        return_errno_with_message!(Errno::EINVAL, "the `O_DIRECT` flag is not supported");
   270	    }
   271	
   272	    // TODO: Setting most of the other flags will succeed on Linux, but their effects need to be
   273	    // validated.
   274	
   275	    Ok(())
   276	}
   277	
   278	impl Drop for PipeWriter {
   279	    fn drop(&mut self) {
   280	        self.state.shutdown();
   281	    }
   282	}
   283	
   284	#[cfg(ktest)]
   285	mod test {
   286	    use alloc::sync::Arc;
   287	    use core::sync::atomic::{self, AtomicBool};
   288	
   289	    use ostd::prelude::*;
   290	
   291	    use super::*;
   292	    use crate::thread::{kernel_thread::ThreadOptions, Thread};
   293	
   294	    #[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
   295	    enum Ordering {
